  │  51 +     )                                                            │

  │  57 +     # Outcomes must match for determinism                        │
  │  58 +     assert run1.outcome == run2.outcome, "Non-deterministic pip… │
  │  59 +     assert run1.artifacts == run2.artifacts, "Artifact mismatch… │
  │  60 +                                                                  │
  │  61 +     return True                                                  │
  │  62 +                                                                  │
  │  63 +                                                                  │

  │  65 +     """Verify that applying infrastructure config multiple times │
  │  66 +     produces the same end state (no drift on re-apply).          │
  │  67 +     """                                                          │
  │  68 +     checker = D_DEVOPSChecker()                                  │
  │  69 +                                                                  │
  │  70 +     resources = [                                                │
  │  71 +         InfrastructureResource(                                  │

  │  37 +     def analyze_domain(self, domain_dir: Path) -> Optional[Doma… │

  │  43 +                                                                  │

  │  46 +         line_count = 0                                           │
  │  47 +         invariant_count = 0                                      │

  │  58 +                     if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef):        │
  │  59 +                         if node.name.startswith("check_"):       │
  │  60 +                             invariant_count += 1                 │
  │  61 +                             key_invariants.append(node.name)     │
  │  62 +             except SyntaxError:                                  │
  │  63 +                 pass                                             │
  │  64 +                                                                  │
  │  65 +         # Count tests                                            │
  │  66 +         test_dir = domain_dir / "tests"                          │
  │  67 +         test_count = 0                                           │
  │  68 +         if test_dir.exists():                                    │
  │  69 +             test_count = len(list(test_dir.glob("test_*.py")))   │
  │  70 +                                                                  │
  │  71 +         # Determine status                                       │
  │  72 +         if line_count >= 100:                                    │
  │  73 +             status = "deep"                                      │
  │  74 +         elif line_count >= 50:                                   │
  │  75 +             status = "developing"                                │
  │  76 +         elif line_count > 0:                                     │
  │  77 +             status = "stub"                                      │
  │  78 +         else:                                                    │
  │  79 +             status = "empty"                                     │
  │  80 +                                                                  │

  │  86 +             test_count=test_count,                               │

  │  89 +         )                                                        │
